## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

1. `HandleKeyOrTextEvent` is preferred over stale `OnKeyPress`: the physical function occupies the inherited `EventHandler` secondary vtable, consumes the project `Event` layout, checks `kEventKeyDown`, and uses `EventMan::TranslateEventKey`.
2. `m_buttonHighlight` is preferred over generic overlay/pressed-state names: the byte is initialized false, has three direct state-setting callers, is queried by one retained getter, and gates only the `CAPSULE.EPD` frame.
3. `HitTestPart` is preferred over `GetPaneTypeCode`: the same five-byte return body is installed in the terminal ControlPane hit-test slot across ten independent control vtables, and current ControlPane source defines the base method as non-const and returning `10`. The derived inline declaration therefore remains non-const; adding `const` would create a different overload instead of overriding the inherited slot.
4. `ObjectStatusBlob` should publicly derive from `HumanImageRenderParams`: the prefix is byte-for-byte and field-for-field identical through `0x40`, the larger record appends only face shape plus three tail bytes, and NewHuman calls pass the larger record's address directly without conversion or copy. This is more plausible human source than repeated casts or duplicate conflicting fields.
5. `ObjectImageButtonPane` belongs in this file, not `SpecializedButtonPanes.cpp`: it directly derives from the base image control, its constructor calls the base at `0x00520820`, its vtables immediately follow the base vtables, and `SpecializedButtonPanes` already treats it as an exclusion/cross-reference.
6. Implicit virtual destructors are preferred over handwritten bodies: every ObjectImage destructor artifact contains inherited teardown and compiler delete-flag lowering, with no distinct source-authored cleanup.
7. MonsterImageLib's two `0x20` caches are typed `stdext::hash_map` members, not project-authored `DATIndexVector` members. Constructor, lookup/insert, cleanup, and old-MSVC identical-COMDAT lowering establish `stdext::hash_map<int, DATFile *>` at `+0x1c` and `stdext::hash_map<int, MonsterArchiveBoundsBucket *>` at `+0x3c`; historical DATIndexVector labels remain search evidence only.
8. `RenderMonster` is part of the required MonsterImageLib declaration: fresh bounded analysis at `0x004db100` reports size `0x229`, one caller at `0x0053bda8`, and the source argument order `(GrafPort *, const RectBounds *, const ObjectStatusBlob *, unsigned int animationGroup, unsigned int frameIndex)` with void use.

## Evidence Standards Used

- Strong: exact bytes/hash, instruction-level branch/call/field behavior, full xref set, vtable slot identity, complete stack frame, an exact freshly reread generated API at the applicable gate, or an independent matching consumer.
- Medium-high: source-facing name or historical header split inferred from several strong structural facts without symbols.
- Historical only: previous report/session/generated snapshots not freshly revalidated.
- Rejected: decompiler labels, generated code contradicted by binary/current headers, raw pointer arithmetic as final source, and broad aggregate ownership based only on adjacency.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- Seven authored functions form a coherent base/subclass source family.
- Base and button sizes are independently proved by field offsets and scalar-delete sizes.
- Six decorated vtables are contiguous and point to the exact authored/compiler functions.
- Constructor call spread proves broad reusable-control ownership.
- Current callback-applied project headers and support documents close every identified runtime API and source-channel dependency. Residual caps are limited to original lexical spelling and absent rebuilt-binary/compile parity, not active support defects.
- Resource strings, event constants, visual states, delay, image branches, render arguments, and cleanup asymmetry are instruction-level facts.
- The 2026-08-22 transaction-copy sweeps and later isolated receipts remain dated history. Fresh current-canonical session `supervisor_canonical_research_20260824_0215` now independently confirms every F01-F08/G01-G06 function name, persisted/public/decompiler type, frame, four comment channels, range/size/body hash, collision guard, xref set, and callee set, plus all six protected vtable heads, all 82 cells, both resource entities, and both protected global/storage entities. G01-G04 return `_DWORD *__thiscall(_DWORD *this, char)` in both public channels plus raw-name `Pane *` decompilation, with exact comments, frames, item/range classifications, and singleton xrefs. It performed no action and does not infer post-R/post-Q presentation.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

1. **Accepted: standalone ObjectImageControlPane source pair.** Broad constructor reuse, direct base/subclass relation, contiguous authored core, contiguous vtables, and independent status/image dependencies support this.
2. **Rejected: HeadSelectDialog ownership.** HeadSelect is one constructor/setter consumer among many and does not own base preview behavior.
3. **Rejected: menu-dialog ownership.** Text/item/spell/menu constructors are callers; their compiler glue is merely interleaved after this range.
4. **Rejected: SpecializedButtonPanes ownership.** That file owns unrelated Gender/Direction/CheckBox controls and already excludes ObjectImage source.
5. **Rejected: Monster/NewHuman ownership.** They own render libraries and descriptors, not the UI control.

### Parser availability and tagged-type handling

The 2026-08-18 `type_inspect`/`type_query` recheck is explicit rather than inferred from pseudocode. `ObjectImageControlPane` and `ObjectImageButtonPane` exist only as incomplete `kind:other` names with no UDT layout, so every T-stage signature that names either class uses an explicit `struct` tag. `ObjectStatusBlob` (`0x44`), `RectBounds` (`0x10`), `Event` (`0x110`), `ControlPane` (`0x108`), and `TimerHandler` (`0x04`) are concrete UDTs. The exact `EventHandler` query has pointer-kind presentation rather than a concrete exact-name UDT row, but parser availability is directly proven by current persisted `LineInputPane__HandleKeyOrTextEvent` at `0x004f25a0`, whose type is exactly `bool __thiscall(EventHandler *this, Event *event)`. TimerHandler availability is independently proven by current persisted `UserPane__OnTimer` at `0x005a83b0` and `TimerHandler__ScheduleTimer` at `0x005975e0`. Handler and concrete support names therefore use those proven normalized unqualified forms; only the absent ObjectImage class names use explicit incomplete tags. No T stage depends on an unqualified absent class name, and no report action proposes creating or expanding either ObjectImage UDT.

Fresh I01 parser evidence adds one syntax requirement without changing those type decisions: public `set_type` requires a named function declarator, not an anonymous function type. Every exact request below therefore places that row's desired function name between `__thiscall` and the parameter list. The name-bearing request string is not an expected public type rendering; `inspect_items.type` and `analyze_function.prototype` may normalize it to the separately listed no-name strings under `### Exact normalized post-type readbacks`.

### Atomic action and readback contract

For F01-F08 use four sequential action/cache stages per row, and complete each row through C before beginning the next row. R is name-only and must use `rename({database:DB,batch:{func:{addr:<address>,name:<name>},pure:true,allow_overwrite:false,stop_on_error:true}})`. Q is the exact-function cache-only refresh `force_recompile({database:DB,items:{addr:<address>}})` using the same row address; omitted `items`, an empty list, a different address, or more than one item is forbidden. T is one `set_type({database:DB,edits:{addr:<address>,signature:<exact named function-declarator request payload from the table>}})`; the request name must equal the row's desired name. C is one `set_address_repeatable_comments({database:DB,items:{addr:<address>,comment:<comment>}})`. Each row is classified `incorporate`. Before R, `lookup_funcs({database:DB,queries:<name>})` must return `Not found`. A row's Q readback is evaluated with every accepted predecessor T already present; it is not an independent replay from the original fourteen-row prestate.

The live 2026-08-18 public MCP schema advertises `force_recompile` as invalidating the Hex-Rays cache for one or more exact function-entry addresses and warns that omitted or empty `items` recompiles every function. This contract authorizes only the one-address form above. Q is not permission for broad analysis, auto-analysis, global reanalysis, function discovery, function creation, type propagation, or any IDB mutation outside the exact renamed function's decompiler cache. Any unexpected persisted/public type, frame, comment, byte, xref, range, vtable, resource, or other item-state change is an unsaved hard stop.

The fourteen function rows therefore contain exactly 56 ordered action/cache endpoint stages: 14 R, 14 Q, 14 named-declarator T, and 14 C. They also require 14 pre-R collision-guard requests. Immediate row readback is exactly 322 requests: 70 after R (five per row: current-schema one-address `inspect_items({database:DB,addrs:[<row-address>]})`, `analyze_function`, `stack_frame`, `get_comments`, and `xrefs_to`) plus 252 after Q, T, and C (six per row after each stage, adding `decompile`). Including collision guards and action/cache stages, the entity-row topology remains exactly 392 public endpoint requests. The current G01-G04 repair changes zero endpoint counts and zero T payload/readback fields: nine rows retain their existing prospective presentation, I03 has the observed predecessor-refined post-Q decompiler declaration, and I09-I12 now require literal fresh row-entry/post-R/post-Q recording rather than fixed inferred type strings. The fresh current-canonical preflight performed none of the 14 collision guards or 56 action/cache stages. The fourteen named function-declarator requests and twenty-eight normalized post-T fields remain separate, one `inspect_items.type` plus one `analyze_function.prototype` value per row. Fresh runtime and persistence requests belong to the supervisor's external Gate 2B procedure and are not folded into this entity-row total; the separately bounded initial/final P01-P04 and function-body protection sweeps remain additional entity-level protection reads.

### Exact normalized post-type readbacks

### `ObjectImageControlPane.cpp`

RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE
```cpp
#include "ObjectImageControlPane.h"

#include "../core/DialogPane.h"
#include "../core/Event.h"
#include "../core/ScreenPane.h"
#include "../../render/EPFTileContext.h"
#include "../../render/GrafPort.h"
#include "../../render/ImageLib.h"
#include "../../render/ItemObjImageLib.h"
#include "../../render/MonsterImageLib.h"
#include "../../render/NewHumanImageLib.h"
#include "../../render/Surface.h"
#include <windows.h>

ObjectImageControlPane::ObjectImageControlPane(
    const ObjectStatusBlob *objectStatus, const RectBounds *bounds)
    : ControlPane(8, bounds)
{
    m_objectStatus.m_displayStatusByte = 0x50;
    m_objectStatus = *objectStatus;
}

void ObjectImageControlPane::OnPaint()
{
    EPFTileContext tileContext;
    RectBounds imageBounds;
    RectBounds destinationBounds;

    SetDrawColor(0);
    g_pfnFillRect(this, &m_visibleBounds);

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
    {
        g_pNewHumanImageLib->CalculateBounds(
            &m_objectStatus, 2, NULL, 0, &imageBounds, NULL, 0, true, 0);

        const int width = imageBounds.right - imageBounds.left;
        const int height = imageBounds.bottom - imageBounds.top;
        destinationBounds.left =
            (m_visibleBounds.left + m_visibleBounds.right - width) / 2;
        destinationBounds.top =
            (m_visibleBounds.top + m_visibleBounds.bottom - height) / 2;
        destinationBounds.right = destinationBounds.left + width;
        destinationBounds.bottom = destinationBounds.top + height;

        g_pNewHumanImageLib->Draw(
            this, &destinationBounds, &m_objectStatus, 2, NULL, 0, 0,
            false, NULL, 0.0f, 0, true, 0);
        return;
    }

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::MonsterObject)
    {
        g_pMonsterImageLib->ComputeRenderBounds(
            &m_objectStatus, 3, 0, &imageBounds, NULL);

        const int width = imageBounds.right - imageBounds.left;
        const int height = imageBounds.bottom - imageBounds.top;
        destinationBounds.left =
            (m_visibleBounds.left + m_visibleBounds.right - width) / 2;
        destinationBounds.top =
            (m_visibleBounds.top + m_visibleBounds.bottom - height) / 2;
        destinationBounds.right = destinationBounds.left + width;
        destinationBounds.bottom = destinationBounds.top + height;

        g_pMonsterImageLib->RenderMonsterImage(
            this, &destinationBounds, &m_objectStatus, 3, 0,
            0, 0, NULL, 0.0f);
        return;
    }

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::ItemObject)
    {
        g_pItemObjImageLib->GetItemGlyphBounds(
            m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId, &imageBounds);

        const int width = imageBounds.right - imageBounds.left;
        const int height = imageBounds.bottom - imageBounds.top;
        destinationBounds.left =
            (m_visibleBounds.left + m_visibleBounds.right - width) / 2;
        destinationBounds.top =
            (m_visibleBounds.top + m_visibleBounds.bottom - height) / 2;
        destinationBounds.right = destinationBounds.left + width;
        destinationBounds.bottom = destinationBounds.top + height;

        g_pItemObjImageLib->DrawItemImage(
            this, &destinationBounds, m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId,
            m_objectStatus.m_variantOrPalette, NULL, 0.0f);
        tileContext.ReleaseBuffers();
    }
}

ObjectImageButtonPane::ObjectImageButtonPane(
    const ObjectStatusBlob *objectStatus, const RectBounds *bounds)
    : ObjectImageControlPane(objectStatus, bounds),
      m_buttonHighlight(false)
{
    m_controlActive = false;
    m_controlFlags = 1;
    m_handlesEnterKey = true;
}

bool ObjectImageButtonPane::HandleKeyOrTextEvent(Event *event)
{
    const unsigned char key = g_pEventMan->TranslateEventKey(
        event->m_payload.m_key.m_key,
        event->m_payload.m_key.m_modifiers);

    if (event->m_type != kEventKeyDown ||
        (key != ' ' && key != '\r'))
        return false;

    DialogPane *dialog = static_cast<DialogPane *>(GetParentPane());
    const int controlIndex = dialog->FindControlIndex(this);

    dialog->SetSelectionVisualState(controlIndex, 11);
    g_pScreenPane->RenderPresentation();
    Sleep(0x85);
    dialog->SetSelectionVisualState(-1, 10);
    g_pScreenPane->RenderPresentation();
    dialog->OnControlCommand(controlIndex, 11);
    return true;
}

void ObjectImageButtonPane::OnPaint()
{
    EPFTileContext tileContext;
    RectBounds imageBounds;
    RectBounds destinationBounds;

    if (m_buttonHighlight)
    {
        g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"CAPSULE.EPD", 0, &tileContext);

        const int width =
            tileContext.bounds.right - tileContext.bounds.left;
        const int height =
            tileContext.bounds.bottom - tileContext.bounds.top;
        destinationBounds.left =
            (m_visibleBounds.left + m_visibleBounds.right - width) / 2;
        destinationBounds.top =
            (m_visibleBounds.top + m_visibleBounds.bottom - height) / 2;
        destinationBounds.right = destinationBounds.left + width;
        destinationBounds.bottom = destinationBounds.top + height;

        RenderTileFrame(
            &tileContext, &tileContext.bounds, &destinationBounds,
            0, L"NPAL3.PAL", NULL);
    }

    g_pNewHumanImageLib->CalculateBounds(
        &m_objectStatus, 2, NULL, 0, &imageBounds, NULL, 0, true, 0);

    const int width = imageBounds.right - imageBounds.left;
    const int height = imageBounds.bottom - imageBounds.top;
    destinationBounds.left =
        (m_visibleBounds.left + m_visibleBounds.right - width) / 2;
    destinationBounds.top =
        (m_visibleBounds.top + m_visibleBounds.bottom - height) / 2;
    destinationBounds.right = destinationBounds.left + width;
    destinationBounds.bottom = destinationBounds.top + height;

    g_pNewHumanImageLib->Draw(
        this, &destinationBounds, &m_objectStatus, 2, NULL, 0, 0,
        false, NULL, 0.0f, 0, true, 0);
}

void ObjectImageButtonPane::SetButtonHighlight(bool highlighted)
{
    m_buttonHighlight = highlighted;
}

bool ObjectImageButtonPane::IsButtonHighlighted() const
{
    return m_buttonHighlight;
}
```
